- **rigor-bug-hunt**: Use when you want REAL bugs found and proven, not a list of guesses. Each candidate is proven with a failing test. The flagship. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-bug-hunt.md))
- **rigor-consistency-closure**: Use when the same concept is implemented divergently and you want it closed for good, in one canonical form, mechanically enforced. - **rigor-deep-review**: Use when you want a PR or diff reviewed at the verification bar, blocking only on CONFIRMED defects and regressions. The high-rigor counterpart to /code-ops-suite-pr-review. - **rigor-fix-verified**: Use when CONFIRMED bugs exist and you want them fixed at root cause with proof. Requires CONFIRMED findings as input. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-fix-verified.md))
- **rigor-ground-truth**: Use when you want the factual baseline before any analysis. Runs the real toolchain and captures ground truth plus a coverage and blind-spot map. Run this first. - **rigor-improve-measured**: Use when you want measured, behavior-preserving improvements, not speculative refactors. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-improve-measured.md))
- **rigor-quality-scan**: Use when you want high-signal, defect-causing quality issues with evidence and tiers, not cosmetic nits. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-quality-scan.md))
- **rigor-regression-hunt**: Use when something used to work and you need to pinpoint the commit that broke it and find related regressions in recent changes. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-regression-hunt.md))
- **rigor-review**: The weekly ≤10-min rigor review (manual learning loop): review the week's build sessions, judge scores, DoD waivers, and outcomes against the value-action registry; spot recurring corrections; propose→approve a codified fix so feedback becomes durable improvement. - **rigor-rigor-sweep**: Use when you want the whole rigor suite run end-to-end as a checkpointed pipeline. Start on the assess-only track. Intra-plugin orchestrator. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-rigor-sweep.md))
- **rigor-safety-net**: Use before refactoring or fixing low-coverage code. Writes characterization tests that lock current observable behavior. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-safety-net.md))
- **rigor-test-suite-audit**: Use when you need to know whether a green suite actually catches faults. Validates the tests other proofs rest on. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/opencode-dist/commands/rigor-test-suite-audit.md))
